I guess it's possible. \n\nI mean, I organize things for a living, so... I didn't feel particularly stressed by it. It's just... that's around the time I noticed it. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Has a doctor told you that you have any medical condition that could be affecting your mood or energy?\n\nPatient: No, um... I haven't been to a doctor for anything like that. I don't really think about it like that.\n\nDoctor: During this past year, was there ever a period of at least two weeks when you felt depressed most of the day nearly every day?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. Not like that.\n\nDoctor: Over the past year, have you often felt tired or low on energy?\n\nPatient: No... not really. My energy is fine, I guess.
